import qiskit
from qiskit import QuantumCircuit, ClassicalRegister, QuantumRegister
from qiskit.quantum_info import Statevector
from qiskit.aqua import QuantumCircuitTransformer
from qiskit.aqua.algorithms import QAOA
from qiskit.aqua.operators import X, Y, Z

def create_quantum_entanglement(qr):
    qc = QuantumCircuit(qr)
    qc.h(qr[0])
    qc.cx(qr[0], qr[1])
    return qc

qr = QuantumRegister(2)
qc = create_quantum_entanglement(qr)
qc.measure(qr, c)

class QuantumEntanglementTransformer(QuantumCircuitTransformer):
    def transform(self, qc):
        return qc

def train_qnix(images, labels):
    optimizer = 'ADAM'
    num_layers = 5
    num_iterations = 100
    num_qubits = 4

    backend = qiskit.Aer.get_backend('qasm_simulator')
    shots = 1024

    qaoa = QAOA(optimizer, num_layers)
    qaoa.set_parameters(num_qubits)
    qaoa.compile(backend)

    for i in range(num_iterations):
        qaoa.update_parameters()
        qaoa.run(images, labels)

    return qaoa.get_statevector()

import qiskit
from qiskit import QuantumCircuit, ClassicalRegister, QuantumRegister
from qiskit.quantum_info import Statevector
from qiskit.aqua import QuantumCircuitTransformer
from qiskit.aqua.algorithms import QAOA
from qiskit.aqua.algorithms import SimulatedAnnealing
from qiskit.aqua.algorithms import SPSA
from qiskit.aqua.algorithms import VQE
from qiskit.aqua.algorithms import COBYLA

def create_quantum_entanglement(qr):
    qc = QuantumCircuit(qr)
    qc.h(qr[0])
    qc.cx(qr[0], qr[1])
    return qc

qr = QuantumRegister(2)
qc = create_quantum_entanglement(qr)
qc.measure(qr, c)

class QuantumEntanglementTransformer(QuantumCircuitTransformer):
    def transform(self, qc):
        return qc

def train_qnix(images, labels):
    optimizer = 'ADAM'
    num_layers = 5
    num_iterations = 100
    num_qubits = 4

    backend = qiskit.Aer.get_backend('qasm_simulator')
    shots = 1024

    qaoa = QAOA(optimizer, num_layers)
    qaoa.set_parameters(num_qubits)
    qaoa.compile(backend)

    for i in range(num_iterations):
        qaoa.update_parameters()
        qaoa.run(images, labels)

    return qaoa.get_statevector()

images = [...]
labels = [...]
statevector = train_qnix(images, labels)
print(statevector)

class SPQAOA(SimulatedAnnealing):
    def __init__(self, quantum_instance, ansatz, objective, bounds, schedule, initial_state=0, 
                 initial_temp=0.01, num_reads=100, max_iter=1000, num_averages=10, 
                 learning_rate=0.1, use_spsa=True):
        super().__init__(quantum_instance, ansatz, objective, bounds, schedule, initial_state, 
                         initial_temp, num_reads, max_iter, num_averages)
        self.use_spsa = use_spsa
        self.learning_rate = learning_rate

    def _update_parameters(self, x, temp):
        if self.use_spsa:
            self.params = self.spsa_update(self.params, x, temp)
        else:
            self.params = self.sa_update(self.params, x, temp)

    def spsa_update(self, params, x, temp):
        # SPSA UPDATE
        return params + 0.1 * (x - params) / temp

    def sa_update(self, params, x, temp):
        # SA UPDATE
        return params + 0.1 * (x - params)
